00054: def attach(parent, key, node):
00055:     assert key not in parent.members, key
00056:     return parent.include(key, node)
00057: 
00058: def block(size, center, color):
00059:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00060:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00061:     return box(size, center=center, color=color)
00062: 
00063: def rounded(size, radius, center, color):
00064:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00065:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and 0 < radius <= min(size)/2
00066:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00067:     return fillet_box(size, radius=radius, center=center, color=color)
00068: 
00069: def line(radius, start, end, color):
00070:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00071:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*start,*end))
00072:     assert sum((a-b)**2 for a,b in zip(start,end)) > 1e-16
00073:     return rod(radius, start=start, end=end, color=color)
00074: 
00075: def tube(points, radius, color):
00076:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00077:     assert len(points) >= 2
00078:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for p in points for v in p)
00079:     assert all(sum((a-b)**2 for a,b in zip(p,q)) > 1e-16
00080:                for p,q in zip(points,points[1:]))
00081:     return sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color)
00082: 
00083: def sphere(radius, center, color):
00084:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00085:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00086:     return ball(radius, center=center, color=color)

00291: def subtree_meshes(parent_name):
00292:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(parent_name)
00293:     assert parent is not None, parent_name
00294:     stack,meshes = [parent],[]
00295:     while stack:
00296:         obj = stack.pop()
00297:         if obj.type == 'MESH':
00298:             meshes.append(obj)
00299:         stack.extend(list(obj.children))
00300:     assert meshes, parent_name
00301:     return meshes
00302: 
00303: def local_roughness(parent_name,value):
00304:     copies = {}
00305:     for obj in subtree_meshes(parent_name):
00306:         for slot in obj.material_slots:
00307:             original = slot.material
00308:             if original is None:
00309:                 continue
00310:             key = original.as_pointer()
00311:             if key not in copies:
00312:                 mat = original.copy()
00313:                 mat.name = f'{parent_name} / {original.name}'
00314:                 mat.use_nodes = True
00315:                 bsdf = mat.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00316:                 if bsdf is not None:
00317:                     bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = value
00318:                 copies[key] = mat
00319:             slot.material = copies[key]

00322:                    ('chaise',.79),('pillow',.90),
00323:                    ('label',.79),('foreground chair',.79)]:

00054:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00055:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00056:     return box(size, center=center, color=color)

00059: def rounded(size, radius, center, color):
00060:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00061:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and 0 < radius <= min(size) / 2
00062:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00063:     return fillet_box(size, radius=radius, center=center, color=color)
00064:

00066: def line(radius, start, end, color):
00067:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00068:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*start, *end))
00069:     assert sum((a - b) ** 2 for a, b in zip(start, end)) > 1e-16
00070:     return rod(radius, start=start, end=end, color=color)
00071: 
00072: 
00073: def tube(points, radius, color):
00074:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00075:     assert len(points) >= 2
00076:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for p in points for v in p)
00077:     assert all(
00078:         sum((a - b) ** 2 for a, b in zip(p, q)) > 1e-16
00079:         for p, q in zip(points, points[1:])
00080:     )
00081:     return sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color)
00082: 
00083:

00085:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0

00336: # Constructors validate dimensions, fillet radii and distinct path vertices.
00337: # attach() validates unique include keys within each parent.

00340: # Resolve material targets through exact named EMPTY subtrees.
00341: # Copy cached SceneAct materials before semantic-local shader customization.
00342: def subtree_meshes(parent_name):
00343:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(parent_name)
00344:     assert parent is not None, f'Missing semantic parent: {parent_name}'
00345:     meshes = []
00346:     stack = [parent]
00347:     while stack:
00348:         obj = stack.pop()
00349:         if obj.type == 'MESH':
00350:             meshes.append(obj)
00351:         stack.extend(list(obj.children))
00352:     assert meshes, f'Empty semantic subtree: {parent_name}'
00353:     return meshes
00354: 
00355: 
00356: def set_local_roughness(parent_name, roughness):
00357:     copies = {}
00358:     for obj in subtree_meshes(parent_name):
00359:         for slot in obj.material_slots:
00360:             original = slot.material
00361:             if original is None:
00362:                 continue
00363:             key = original.as_pointer()
00364:             if key not in copies:
00365:                 material = original.copy()
00366:                 material.name = f'{parent_name} / {original.name}'
00367:                 material.use_nodes = True
00368:                 bsdf = material.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00369:                 if bsdf is not None:
00370:                     bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = roughness
00371:                 copies[key] = material
00372:             slot.material = copies[key]
00373: 
00374: 
00375: for parent_name, roughness in [
00376:     ('architecture', .79),
00377:     ('window', .90),
00378:     ('chaise', .79),
00379:     ('pillow', .90),
00380:     ('label', .79),
00381:     ('foreground chair', .79),
00382: ]:
00383:     set_local_roughness(parent_name, roughness)
00384:

00388: if world is None:

00390:     bpy.context.scene.world = world

00392: background = world.node_tree.nodes.get('Background')

00052: def attach(parent, key, node):
00053:     assert key not in parent.members, key
00054:     return parent.include(key, node)

00056: 
00057: def block(size, center, color):
00058:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00059:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00060:     return box(size, center=center, color=color)
00061: 
00062: 
00063: def rounded(size, radius, center, color):
00064:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00065:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and 0 < radius <= min(size) / 2
00066:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00067:     return fillet_box(size, radius=radius, center=center, color=color)
00068: 
00069: 
00070: def line(radius, start, end, color):
00071:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00072:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*start, *end))
00073:     assert sum((a-b)**2 for a, b in zip(start, end)) > 1e-16
00074:     return rod(radius, start=start, end=end, color=color)
00075: 
00076: 
00077: def tube(points, radius, color):
00078:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00079:     assert len(points) >= 2
00080:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for p in points for v in p)
00081:     assert all(sum((a-b)**2 for a, b in zip(p, q)) > 1e-16
00082:                for p, q in zip(points, points[1:]))
00083:     return sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color)
00084: 
00085: 
00086: def sphere(radius, center, color):
00087:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00088:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00089:     return ball(radius, center=center, color=color)

00284: 
00285: def subtree_meshes(parent_name):
00286:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(parent_name)
00287:     assert parent is not None, parent_name
00288:     stack, meshes = [parent], []
00289:     while stack:
00290:         obj = stack.pop()
00291:         if obj.type == 'MESH':
00292:             meshes.append(obj)
00293:         stack.extend(list(obj.children))
00294:     assert meshes, parent_name
00295:     return meshes
00296: 
00297: 
00298: def local_roughness(parent_name, value):
00299:     copies = {}
00300:     for obj in subtree_meshes(parent_name):
00301:         for slot in obj.material_slots:
00302:             original = slot.material
00303:             if original is None:
00304:                 continue
00305:             key = original.as_pointer()
00306:             if key not in copies:
00307:                 mat = original.copy()
00308:                 mat.name = f'{parent_name} / {original.name}'
00309:                 mat.use_nodes = True
00310:                 bsdf = mat.node_tree.nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00311:                 if bsdf is not None:
00312:                     bsdf.inputs['Roughness'].default_value = value
00313:                 copies[key] = mat
00314:             slot.material = copies[key]
00315:

00318:                     ('chaise', .79), ('pillow', .90),
00319:                     ('label', .79), ('foreground chair', .79)]:
00320:     local_roughness(name, value)
00321:

00323:     for obj in subtree_meshes(name):
00324:         if len(obj.data.polygons) > 12:
00325:             for polygon in obj.data.polygons:
00326:                 polygon.use_smooth = True
